| infographics/icbm-missile-silo.png | Claude (Anthropic) via tools/asset-gen/infographics/build.py (pages_icbm_silo.py) | HTML laid out in Python, PNG via Playwright; 3D renders of the shipped missile body and warhead OBJ models standing in the silo (HBM's Nuclear Tech Mod contributors, LGPL-3.0, modified; see missile_body_t3 and warhead_t3 above) by tools/asset-gen/infographics/render3d.py; includes GregTech 5 Unofficial textures (hulls, machine overlays, plate icons) read from the pack's GT jar; the silo is drawn from the mod's own Gantry Casing and Gantry Beam art with Galacticraft Legacy's launch pad (MIT, D85) and the NewHorizonsCoreMod Heavy Duty Rocket Engine and Fins Tier 3 icons (GPL-3.0, D86); the ground, sand, logs, stone and dirt are the real Minecraft textures, grass and leaves tinted green as the game does, read from the Minecraft 1.7.10 client jar at build time and never stored in the repo (D55); numbers read from the code | **GPL-3.0** as a combined work with the NewHorizonsCoreMod icons it paints in (credited in its footer); its other parts keep their own licenses: our art MIT, the Galacticraft art MIT, the NTM art LGPL-3.0, the GregTech hatch overlays LGPL-3.0, the Minecraft textures © Mojang / Microsoft | 2026-10-26 |

## Third-party assets

Human-made files that are not ours, under CC0, CC-BY, MIT or LGPL (ICBM module; rules in [CONTRIBUTING § Assets and localization](../../CONTRIBUTING.md#assets-and-localization) and the [third-party asset folder](../../src/main/resources/assets/gregguns/thirdparty/README.md)). Each file keeps its own license, with the license text beside it. Third-party code is listed in [THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md](../../THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md) instead.
